  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Secret Rares</strong></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Beginning with Scarlet &amp; Violet base set, Full Art cards are now also considered secret rares. These cards will have a collector number above that of the number of cards in the set, e.g. 201/198.</p>
